Original scores fifteen of sixteen; this VQ2.1/dense4 mixture scores fourteen. Both miss sort-records. The mixture additionally fails filter-unique by returning all unique values instead of filtering negative values: original [-3, -8, -1], mixture [2, -3, 0, -8, 4, -1]. All tested coding, tools, multilingual and retrieval outcomes remain passing. Every answer and deterministic grade is preserved.

The mixture misses its prospectively frozen minimum of fifteen passes, so it does not advance to a speed pilot or product integration. The separately screened affine two-bit gate/up, three-bit down recipe remains a positive lead with original non-expert tensors. Do not loosen this gate after seeing its outcome or tune repeatedly against these tasks.

The existing complete static suite and native T0/T1 catalogue pass on the source-bound repaired build, including the nonconstant D8 batch/one-row regression. Python overlay tests pass.
